archive.shape[0]))\n   181\t                    base2 = archive[r2_arch]\n   182\t                else:\n   183\t                    base2 = pop[r2]\n   184\t                \n   185\t                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\n   186\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - base2)\n   187\t                \n   188\t                # Binomial crossover (ensure at least one component from mutant)\n   189\t                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\n   190\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n   191\t # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n 80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': np.empty((pop_size, self.dim))\n    98\t            pop[0] = x0_arr[0]\n    99\t            if pop_size > 1:\n   100\t                pop[1:] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size - 1, self.dim))\n   101\t                # Add small local perturbations around x0 to exploit the seed\n   102\t                n_pert = min(pop_size - 1, 2)\n   103\t                pert = 0.05 * (ub - lb) * rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   108\t        \n   109\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   110\t        evals = 0\n   111\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   112\t        \n   113\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   114\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   115\t                break\n   116\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n pop_size))\n   131\t        mu_F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n   132\t        mu_CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n   133\t        p_best_rate = 0.15\n   134\t\n   135\t        # Main 'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   145\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   146\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   147\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   148\t            \n   149\t            # If a controller supplies F/CR, use them as generation-level means\n   150\t            if 'F' in params:\n   151\t                mu_F = float(params['F'])\n   152\t            if 'CR' in params:\n   153\t                mu_CR = float(params['CR'])\n   154\t            \n   155\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   156\t            \n   157\t            # Sort by fitness for pbest selection\n   158\t            order = np.argsort(fitness)\n   159\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p_best_rate))\n   160\t            pbest_pool = order[:n_pbest]\n   161\t            \n   162\t            old_pop = pop.copy()\n   163\t            trials = np.empty_like(pop)\n   164\t            F_vals = np.empty(pop_size)\n   165\t            CR_vals = np.empty(pop_size)\n   166\t            \n   167\t            # Generate trial vectors using JADE current-to-pbest/1\n   168\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   169\t                # pbest individual\n   170\t                pbest_idx = int(pbest_pool[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\n   171\t                pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n   172\t                \n   173\t                # Choose two distinct vectors, not i\n   174\t                available = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   175\t                if len(available) < 2:\n   176\t                    r1, r2 = 0, 1 if i != 1 else 2\n   177\t                else:\n   178\t                    r1, r2 = rng.choice(available, size=2, replace=False)\n   179\t                \n   180\t                # Sample F (Cauchy) and CR (normal) around memories\n   181\t                F_i = mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n   182\t                while F_i <= 0.0:\n   183\t                    F_i = mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n   184\t                F_i = min(1.0, F_i)\n   185\t                \n   186\t                CR_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\n   187\t                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\n   188\t                \n   189\t                F_vals[i] = F_i\n   190\t                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\n   191\t                \n   192\t                # Optional archive use: sometimes pick r2 from the archive\n   193\t                if archive.shape[0] > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:\n   194\t                    r2_arch = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\n   195\t                    base2 = archive[r2_arch]\n   196\t                else:\n   197\t                    base2 = pop[r2]\n   198\t                \n   199\t                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\n   200\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - base2)\n   201\t                \n   202\t                # Binomial crossover (ensure at least one component from mutant)\n   203\t                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\n   204\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n   205\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   206\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   207\t                \n   208\t                # Boundary handling: clip and re-scatter a small fraction near bounds\n   209\t                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   210\t                trials[i] = trial\n   211\t            \n   212\t            # Evaluate all trials (simultaneous selection)\n   213\t            new_pop = pop.copy()\n   214\t            new_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   215\t            replaced = np.zeros(pop_size, dtype=bool)\n   216\t            successful_F = []\n   217\t            successful_CR = []\n   218\t            \n   219\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   220\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   221\t                    done = True\n   222\t                    break\n   223\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trials[i])\n   224\t                evals += 1\n   225\t                \n   226\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   227\t                    new_pop[i] = trials[i].copy()\n   228\t                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\n   229\t                    replaced[i] = True\n   230\t                    successful_F.append(F_vals[i])\n   231\t                    successful_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n   232\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   233\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   234\t                        gbest = trials[i].copy()\n   235\t            \n   236\t            # Apply selection\n   237\t            pop = new_pop\n   238\t            fitness = new_fitness\n   239\t            \n   240\t            # Update archive with replaced parents\n   241\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   242\t                if replaced[i]:\n   243\t                    parent = old_pop[i]\n   244\t                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_capacity:\n   245\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, parent.reshape(1, -1)])\n   246\t                    else:\n   247\t                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))] = parent\n   248\t            \n   249\t            # Update parameter memories with successful trials\n   250\t            if successful_F:\n   251\t                sum_F = float(np.sum(successful_F))\n   252\t                if sum_F > 0.0:\n   253\t                    mu_F = float(np.sum(np.square(successful_F)) / sum_F)\n   254\t            if successful_CR:\n   255\t                mu_CR = float(np.mean(successful_CR))\n   256\t            \n   257\t            # Slowly adapt pbest proportion: more exploration early, focus later\n   258\t            p_best_rate = max(0.05, min(0.25, 0.18 - 0.10 * min(1.0, iteration / 50.0)))\n   259\t            \n   260\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   261\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   262\t            \n   263\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   264\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   265\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   266\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   267\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   268\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   269\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   270\t            \n   271\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   272\t\n   273\t        self.stats = {\n   274\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   275\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   276\t            'evals': int(evals),\n 9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n    87\t        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n    88\t            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n    89\t        else:\n    90\t            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n    91\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n    92\t        \n    93\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    94\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    95\t        \n    96\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    97\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    98\t            try:\n    99\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n   100\t            except Exception:\n   101\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n   103\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n   104\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   105\t\n   106\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   109\t        try:\n   110\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   111\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   112\t        except Exception:\n   113\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   114\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   115\t        \n   116\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   117\t        try:\n   118\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   119\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   120\t        except Exception:\n   121\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   122\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   123\t        \n   124\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   125\t\n   126\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   127\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   128\t\n   129\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   130\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   131\t\n   132\t\n   133\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   134\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   135\t\n   136\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   137\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   138\t    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n   139\t    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n   140\t\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n    87\t        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n    88\t            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n    89\t        else:\n    90\t            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n    91\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n    92\t        \n    93\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    94\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    95\t        \n    96\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    97\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    98\t            try:\n    99\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n   100\t            except Exception:\n   101\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n   103\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n   104\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   105\t\n   106\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   107\t\n   108\t        #      for fi in self.F_vals:\n   177\t            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n   178\t                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n   179\t        self.n_actions = seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Training setup\n   185\t        self.training = training\n   186\t        self.update_freq = update_freq\n   187\t        self.batch_size = epsilon_decay\n   202\t        if training:\n   203\t            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n   204\t            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   205\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   206\t            self.optimizer = torch.Tensor:\n   221\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n   222\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n   223\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n   224\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n   225\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n   226\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n   227\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n   228\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n   232\t        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n   233\t            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n   234\t        else:\n   235\t            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n   236\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n   237\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   238\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   239\t\n   240\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   241\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   242\t\n   243\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   244\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   245\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   246\t        else:\n   247\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   248\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   249\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   250\t\n   251\t        if self.training:\n   252\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   253\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   254\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   255\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   256\t\n   257\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   258\t\n   259\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   260\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   261\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   262\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   263\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   264\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   286\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   287\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   288\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   289\t\n   290\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   291\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   292\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   293\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   294\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   295\t\n   296\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   297\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   298\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   299\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   300\t\n   301\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   302\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   303\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   304\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   305\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   306\t\n   307\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   308\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   309\t\n   310\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   311\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   312\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   313\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   314\t\n   315\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   316\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   317\t        loss.backward()\n   318\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   319\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   320\t\n   321\t        # Update target network periodically\n   322\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   323\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   324\t            # Decay epsilon\n   325\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   326\t\n   327\t        return loss.item()\n   328\t\n   329\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   330\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   331\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   332\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   333\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   334\t        self.reward_history = []\n   335\t\n   336\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   342\t        return self.reward_history\n   343\t\n   344\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   345\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   346\t        return self.total_loss\n   347\t\n   348\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   349\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   350\t        return self.total_reward\n   351\t\n   352\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   353\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), SHADE-style parameter memory\\n        memory_size = 6\\n        F_memory = np.full(memory_size, mu_F)\\n        CR_memory = np.full(memory_size, mu_CR)\\n        mem_pos = 0\\n        stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        def reflect_bounds(x):\\n            \\\"\\\"\\\"Reflect out-of-bound values back into the feasible domain.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n            y = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()\\n            lb_a = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\\n            ub_a = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\\n            width = ub_a - lb_a\\n            y = np.clip(y, lb_a - width, ub_a + width)\\n            below = y < lb_a\\n            above = y > ub_a\\n            y[below] = 2.0 * lb_a[below] - y[below]\\n            y[above] = 2.0 * ub_a[above] - y[above]\\n            y = lb_a + np.mod(y - lb_a, width)\\n            return y\\n\\n        # Sort by fitness for pbest selection\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(cur_pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            pbest_pool = order[:n_pbest]\\n            \\n            old_pop = pop.copy()\\n            trials = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.empty(cur_pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.empty(cur_pop_size)\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE/JADE current-to-pbest/1\\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                # pbest individual\\n                pbest_idx = int(pbest_pool[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                \\n                # Choose two distinct vectors, not i\\n                available = [idx for idx in range(cur_pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(available) < 2:\\n                    r1, r2 = 0, 1\\n                else:\\n                    r1, r2 = rng.choice(available, size=2, replace=False)\\n                \\n                # Select random memory entry for SHADE-style F, CR sampling\\n                mem_i = int(rng.randint(0, memory_size))\\n                F_i = F_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = F_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(1.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = CR_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\\n                \\n                # Optional archive use: sometimes pick r2 from the archive\\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    r2_arch = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\\n                    base2 = archive[r2_arch]\\n                else:\\n                    base2 = pop[r2]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - base2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover (ensure at least one component from mutant)\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect into the feasible box\\n                trial = reflect_bounds(trial)\\n                trials[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate all trials (simultaneous selection)\\n            new_pop = pop.copy()\\n            new_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n            replaced = np.zeros(cur_pop_size, dtype=bool)\\n            successful_F = []\\n            successful_CR = []\\n            improvement_scores = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trials[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    improvement_scores.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\\n                    new_pop[i] = trials[i].copy()\\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    replaced[i] = True\\n                    successful_F.append(F_vals[i])\\n                    successful_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trials[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Apply selection\\n            pop = new_pop\\n            fitness = new_fitness\\n            \\n            # Update archive with replaced parents\\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                if replaced[i]:\\n                    parent = old_pop[i]\\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_capacity:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, parent.reshape(1, -1)])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))] = parent\\n            \\n            # SHADE/JADE weighted parameter memory update\\n            if successful_F and improvement_scores:\\n                weights = np.asarray(improvement_scores, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = float(np.sum(weights))\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones(len(successful_F))\\n                    total_w = float(len(successful_F))\\n                sF = np.asarray(successful_F, dtype=float)\\n                sCR = np.asarray(successful_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\\n                denom = float(np.sum(weights * sF))\\n                if denom > 1e-12:\\n                    F_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.sum(weights * sF * sF) / denom)\\n                else:\\n                    F_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.mean(sF))\\n                CR_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.sum(weights * sCR) / max(1e-12, total_w))\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n                # Keep simple means as well\\n                mu_F = float(np.mean(sF))\\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(sCR))\\n            \\n            # Slowly adapt pbest proportion: more exploration early, focus later\\n            p_best_rate = max(0.05, min(0.25, 0.18 - 0.10 * min(1.0, iteration / 50.0)))\\n            \\n            # Stagnation detection and diversity restoration\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnation_count += 1\\n            \\n            if stagnation_count >= 15 and cur_pop_size > 2:\\n                n_reinit = max(1, int(0.3 * cur_pop_size))\\n                order_stag = np.argsort(fitness)\\n                worst_idx = order_stag[-n_reinit:]\\n                spread = 0.02 * (ub - lb)\\n                new_inds = np.clip(gbest + rng.standard_normal((n_reinit, self.dim)) * spread, lb, ub)\\n                pop[worst_idx] = new_inds\\n                # Evaluate the reinitialized individuals immediately\\n                if evals < self.max_evals:\\n                    for ii, idx in enumerate(worst_idx):\\n                        if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                            break\\n                        fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                        evals += 1\\n                        if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\\n                            gbest_f = fitness[idx]\\n                            gbest = pop[idx].copy()\\n                stagnation_count = 0\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\"\n    }\n}\n</json>"

Update parameter memories with successful trials\\n            if successful_F:\\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(successful_F))\\n                if sum_F > 0.0:\\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(np.square(successful_F)) / sum_F)\\n            if successful_CR:\\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(successful_CR))\\n            \\n            # Slowly adapt pbest proportion: more exploration early, focus later\\n            p_best_rate = max(0.05, min(0.25, 0.18 - 0.10 * min(1.0, iteration / 50.0)))\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\", 'new_str': '        # JADE/SHADE archive and parameter memories\\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\\n        archive_capacity = max(int(2.5 * pop_size), 50)\\n        mu_F = float(self.params.get(\\'F\\', 0.5))\\n        mu_CR = float(self.params.get(\\'CR\\', 0.5))\\n        p_best_rate = 0.15\\n        N_min = max(4, int(0.25 * pop_size))\\n        # SHADE-style parameter memory\\n        memory_size = 6\\n        F_memory = np.full(memory_size, mu_F)\\n        CR_memory = np.full(memory_size, mu_CR)\\n        mem_pos = 0\\n        stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        def reflect_bounds(x):\\n            \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound values back into the feasible domain.\"\"\"\\n            y = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()\\n            lb_a = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\\n            ub_a = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\\n            width = ub_a - lb_a\\n            y = np.clip(y, lb_a - width, ub_a + width)\\n            below = y < lb_a\\n            above = y > ub_a\\n            y[below] = 2.0 * lb_a[below] - y[below]\\n            y[above] = 2.0 * ub_a[above] - y[above]\\n            y = lb_a + np.mod(y - lb_a, width)\\n            return y\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n\\n            # Linear population size reduction (L-SHADE style)\\n            frac = min(1.0, evals / max(1, self.max_evals))\\n            new_size = max(N_min, int(round(pop_size + (N_min - pop_size) * frac)))\\n            if new_size < pop.shape[0]:\\n                order_reduce = np.argsort(fitness)\\n                keep = order_reduce[:new_size]\\n                pop = pop[keep].copy()\\n                fitness = fitness[keep].copy()\\n\\n            cur_pop_size = pop.shape[0]\\n\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {\\'iteration\\': iteration, \\'evals\\': evals, \\'gbest_f\\': gbest_f, \\n                       \\'initial_gbest_f\\': initial_gbest_f, \\'max_evals\\': self.max_evals,\\n                       \\'pop_size\\': cur_pop_size, \\'stagnation_count\\': stagnation_count}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # If a controller supplies F/CR, use them as generation-level means\\n            if \\'F\\' in params:\\n                mu_F = float(params[\\'F\\'])\\n            if \\'CR\\' in params:\\n                mu_CR = float(params[\\'CR\\'])\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Sort by fitness for pbest selection\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(cur_pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            pbest_pool = order[:n_pbest]\\n            \\n            old_pop = pop.copy()\\n            trials = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.empty(cur_pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.empty(cur_pop_size)\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE/JADE current-to-pbest/1\\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                # pbest individual\\n                pbest_idx = int(pbest_pool[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)])\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                \\n                # Choose two distinct vectors, not i\\n                available = [idx for idx in range(cur_pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(available) < 2:\\n                    r1, r2 = 0, 1\\n                else:\\n                    r1, r2 = rng.choice(available, size=2, replace=False)\\n                \\n                # Select random memory entry for SHADE-style F, CR sampling\\n                mem_i = int(rng.randint(0, memory_size))\\n                F_i = F_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = F_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(1.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = CR_memory[mem_i] + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\\n                \\n                # Optional archive use: sometimes pick r2 from the archive\\n                if archive.shape[0] > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    r2_arch = int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))\\n                    base2 = archive[r2_arch]\\n                else:\\n                    base2 = pop[r2]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - base2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover (ensure at least one component from mutant)\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect into the feasible box\\n                trial = reflect_bounds(trial)\\n                trials[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate all trials (simultaneous selection)\\n            new_pop = pop.copy()\\n            new_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n            replaced = np.zeros(cur_pop_size, dtype=bool)\\n            successful_F = []\\n            successful_CR = []\\n            improvement_scores = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trials[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    improvement_scores.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\\n                    new_pop[i] = trials[i].copy()\\n                    new_fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    replaced[i] = True\\n                    successful_F.append(F_vals[i])\\n                    successful_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trials[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Apply selection\\n            pop = new_pop\\n            fitness = new_fitness\\n            \\n            # Update archive with replaced parents\\n            for i in range(cur_pop_size):\\n                if replaced[i]:\\n                    parent = old_pop[i]\\n                    if archive.shape[0] < archive_capacity:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, parent.reshape(1, -1)])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0]))] = parent\\n            \\n            # SHADE/JADE weighted parameter memory update\\n            if successful_F and improvement_scores:\\n                weights = np.asarray(improvement_scores, dtype=float)\\n                total_w = float(np.sum(weights))\\n                if total_w <= 0.0:\\n                    weights = np.ones(len(successful_F))\\n                    total_w = float(len(successful_F))\\n                sF = np.asarray(successful_F, dtype=float)\\n                sCR = np.asarray(successful_CR, dtype=float)\\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\\n                denom = float(np.sum(weights * sF))\\n                if denom > 1e-12:\\n                    F_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.sum(weights * sF * sF) / denom)\\n                else:\\n                    F_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.mean(sF))\\n                CR_memory[mem_pos] = float(np.sum(weights * sCR) / max(1e-12, total_w))\\n                mem_pos = (mem_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n                #     "content": "<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n    \"tool_input\": {\n        \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n        \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/param_controller.py\",\n        \"old_str\": \"class AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\\n\\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\",\n        \"new_str\": \"class SHADEProgressController(ParamController):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Improved SHADE-style parameter controller.\\n\\n    Uses smooth progress-based F/CR scheduling with a memory-like adaptive\\n    component and stagnation-sensitive diversity boosts. This controller is\\n    designed specifically for the L-SHADE/JADE optimizer in ec_algorithm.py.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.25, cr_start=0.95, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 stagnation_boost=0.15, boost_decay=0.9):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.boost_decay = boost_decay\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n        self.active_boost = 0.0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\\n        max_evals = int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000))\\n        evals = int(observation.get('evals', 0))\\n        # Max iterations is estimated based on population size if available\\n        pop_size = int(observation.get('pop_size', 40))\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(max_evals / max(1, pop_size)))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n        # Also use evaluation progress for smoother transition\\n        t_evals = min(1.0, evals / max(1, max_evals))\\n        t = 0.6 * t + 0.4 * t_evals\\n\\n        # Baseline schedule with cosine-like ease\\n        F = self.f_end + (self.f_start - self.f_end) * (1.0 - t) ** 1.0\\n        CR = self.cr_end + (self.cr_start - self.cr_end) * (1.0 - t) ** 0.85\\n\\n        # Use stagnation info provided by the optimizer if available\\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\\n        obs_stagnation = int(observation.get('stagnation_count', 0))\\n        if obs_stagnation > 0:\\n            self.stagnation_count = obs_stagnation\\n        elif current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        if current_gbest is not None:\\n            self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        # Increase diversity as stagnation grows\\n        if self.stagnation_count >= 5:\\n            boost_factor = min(1.0, (self.stagnation_count - 4) / 10.0)\\n            self.active_boost = self.stagnation_boost * boost_factor\\n        else:\\n            self.active_boost = max(0.0, self.active_boost * self.boost_decay)\\n\\n        F = min(1.0, F + self.active_boost)\\n        CR = min(1.0, CR + self.active_boost * 0.7)\\n\\n        # Late-stage refinement: keep F moderate but not too small\\n        if t > 0.8:\\n            F = max(F, 0.15)\\n\\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\\n\\n\\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\\n\\n    -
